# DRAGON RIDER

STRIKER

These aerial combatants dash across the sky, impaling foes with their signature Flying Dragon Style.

D ragon riders are trained to fight against aerial foes, staying mobile and using spears to increase their reach. The best dragon riders bond with the companions (typically dragons) they ride into battle.

This bond isn't just emotional, but magical. When a dragon rider calls, their companion can answer across any distance and adapt to any environment. Their aetheric fields become entwined, and their minds become as one. This special call can only be maintained for a short period of time, before their aetheric fields begin to separate once more.

- Ø What is your companion's name, and what are they like?
- Ø What type of aerial threat were you trained to fight against?
- Ø Where is your companion's natural habitat?
- Ø What do you or your companion fear fighting?

#### LIMIT BREAK

#### **Tempest Storm** Channel Limit Break Range = Scope

**On Channel:** You begin calling a furious storm, and all adjacent characters of your choice are **pushed 1 space** away from you. If you stop channeling this ability before releasing for any reason, you do not expend your **limit break**.

**On Release:** You create a 4 × 4 storm **zone** within Range. The storm lasts until the end of the scene and its spaces are **difficult terrain**.

Characters within the storm when it is created or who enter one of its spaces take 1d6+4 Physical , no more than 1/turn per character. If a character ends their turn in one of the storm's spaces, you may **slide** them up to **3 spaces**.

Ø **Minor Action** (1 MP): You move the storm up to **3 spaces** in a straight line.

*"We thought they was normal storm clouds, though they did seem to form outta nowhere. There wasn't no forecast of rain, neither. But the lightning started immediately, even before the rain. And then the winds hit. You've never felt winds like this. Funnels of dust were everywhere, and they swept us off our feet. When I woke up, the ground was scrubbed clean. No bushes, no grass, just rock."*

Druids are flexible, able to use a combination of Channel and Volley abilities. Druids that seek to command the fauna of their environments tend to choose the Companion support item [\(p. 218\)](#page-217-2) and ranks in summoning classes, while those that seek to transform their bodies take ranks in the **Shapeshifter** class [\(p. 178\)](#page-177-0).

### EQUINOX ARTILLERY

Equinoxes harness celestial aether to empower their destructive spells. Aether of the sun enhances the explosive potential of spells, and aether of the moon enhances the effective range of spells.

E quinoxes pull celestial aether from beyond the bounds of their planet, mixing it with the native aether around them. This celestial aether comes in two types – sun aether and moon aether – though they do not necessarily originate from a literal sun and moon.

Sun aether is dangerous and destructive. Spells empowered with sun aether explode violently. Moon aether, on the other hand, is stable and strong. Spells empowered with moon aether can keep their form over longer distances.

- Ø Which type of aether sun or moon – is easier for you to use, and which one do you fear?
- Ø What spell do you know that others have declared too dangerous to use?
- Ø What celestial event are you waiting for, during which something important will happen? An eclipse, a conjunction, or something else?
- Ø What item helps you control your spells?

#### TRAITS

#### **Day/Night Cycle** Trait

You have two special resources – **Sun** and **Moon** – that each start at 0. You can only have one of these resources at a time, and they reset to 0 at the end of each combat scene.

Ø **Minor Action:** You set your **Sun** or **Moon** at **1** (setting the other at 0), or clear all **Sun** and **Moon**. If you already have **1 Sun** or **1 Moon**, you can instead increase it by **+1**, to a maximum of **2**.

**Sun** and **Moon** have the following effects:

- Ø **Sun:** For each **Sun** you have, your AOE spells and artifact weapons gain +1 to their AOEs (**Line** AOEs have their width increased by +1, instead); however, you must spend +1 MP each time you use one of these spells or weapons. If you can't, you immediately lose all **Sun**.
- Ø **Moon:** For each **Moon** you have, your Scope and Range with **artifact weapons** and **spells** that have a Range increase by +4; however, you take 1 Stress each time you target something with a **spell** or **artifact weapon attack** beyond your normal Scope or the weapon's Range.

#### **Mage Tower** Trait

If you haven't moved since the start of your last turn, you gain both of the following benefits, which don't stack:

- Ø **Surecast:** Your **ranged artifact weapon** and **spell attacks** gain **+1** .
- Ø **Sharpcast:** Your Channel spells impose **+1** on any saves they trigger.

WEAPON SLOTS MAIN

#### LIMIT BREAK

**TetraFlare** Channel

Limit Break Arcing, Mana 1 Range = Scope, Blast 1

If you stop channeling this ability before releasing for any reason, it does not expend your limit break.

**On Release:** One character in the center of the AOE is **auto-hit** by a spell attack for 2d6+2 Force . All other characters in the AOE are **auto-hit** for half of that damage.

You can spend up to +3 MP when you release this ability, increasing the damage by 1d6 Force for each additional MP spent. You may also apply the benefits of any amount of **Sun** and **Moon** to this ability (to a max of 2 each), which affect this ability as if it were a spell. This does not require you to have any **Sun** or **Moon** or spend any amount of either.

*Combining sun and moon aether at the same time results in a cataclysmically dangerous spell. An equinox who knows how to cast this spell, and is still alive, is a master of their craft.*

The Equinox's Day/Night Cycle lets them switch between long-range spells or large AOEs, although the cost for repeatedly using each resource can add up quickly. Equinoxes can focus on increasing their MP or Stress Cap to let them use these effects for longer. Picking up spell attacks from other classes can give an Equinox more versatility.

Characters on the Astral side take **Astral**, characters on the **Lunar** side take Lunar, and characters in the center of the AOE take **Force**. Characters spanning more than one location choose which damage they take.

*The signature spell of the equinox. One hand channels sun aether, the other channels moon aether. The spells are released into each other, merging into pure force energy, and decaying into astral and lunar energy at the sides.*

# GRAVEWALKER

DEFENDER

Manifestations of the slow, lumbering specter of death that follows all living things. Gravewalkers use the power of necromancy to stave off death.

G ravewalkers stride the line between life and death. They have close relationships with death, and use their necromantic magic to protect their allies. When you are friends with a gravewalker, you die when they allow it.

These heavily armored necromancers are nigh unkillable, able to shrug off wounds that would fell any other person. Their shovel is functional and symbolic, sometimes used to literally dig themselves out of their own grave.

- Ø How did you die the first time, and how did you come back to life?
- Ø What does the personification of death look like to you, and what are they like?
- Ø How can you tell when someone has been touched by death?
- Ø How do you travel between the world of the living and the world of the dead?

#### LIMIT BREAK

#### **That Which is Already Dead** Special Limit Break

At any time during a combat scene, you may refuse the call of death. Until the end of the scene, you can't take a fourth Wound and don't make any wound rolls. When you would take your fourth Wound, you instead return to your maximum HP and carry over any excess damage.

*Gravewalkers have walked the path of the dead. They know what lies on the other side of life. To kill a gravewalker is to put them on a path they have already marked before. As long as they can "walk", they can always come back.*

Gravewalkers are slow, and armor can slow them down further. To counteract this, they can provide protection over distance. Gravewalkers can either seek out ranged options to get around their slow speed, or choose heavy weapons to make devastating attacks when they finally reach their foes.

When you are defeated, roll 1d6 at the start of each round. On a 5+, you return to action with full HP and 0 Stress and clear either 1 Wound or 1 Overstress; however, you also become Dazed and become Dazed at the start of each of your turns until you take a **Long Rest**.

*Those that wear this armor cannot die. People have sealed themselves inside such armor to achieve immortality.*

### GUNSLINGER ARTILLERY

Gunslingers are masters of firearms. Their quick hands can draw, fire, and reload in the blink of an eye.

Y ou are the bullet. The trigger is your breath. Breathe in. Squeeze. Breathe out.

Gunslingers are firearms specialists. Their insight into their weapons lets them reload supernaturally fast, recharging or replacing ammo with their own aetheric energies. Gunslingers often modify their weapons and ammunition to suit their style and environment.

- Ø How were you taught to aim without sight?
- Ø What unique modification have you made to your weapons?
- Ø What place have you sworn never to return, and why?
- Ø What is your motto or code?

When you **reload** a weapon, you gain the Quick Draw skill until you use it, or until start of your next turn.

Skill

#### LIMIT BREAK

#### **Customized Ammo** Free Action Limit Break

You gain access to the following **special ammunition types** for the rest of the scene. Each ammunition type can be used once. You can choose to use an ammunition type when attacking with a **ranged weapon**, giving the listed effects. The attack's normal effects still apply.

- Ø **Hotshot:** Draw a straight line between yourself and your target, and place a Blast 2 AOE centered anywhere along this line. Characters in the AOE must make an Agility save. **Failure:** They take 2d6 Astral . **Success:** They take half damage. Additionally, the area becomes **dangerous terrain** (Agility, 5 Astral ) until the end of the scene
- Ø **Mindjack Round: On Hit:** Until the end of the target's next turn or until they are dealt damage by you or one of your allies, they consider you and your allies as their allies, and their former allies as enemies. **On Miss:** The target is unable to target you with hostile effects until the end of their next turn.
- Ø **Piercer:** The attack gains **Piercing** and becomes a **Line** attack with a distance of any amount up to the attack's Range. The attack also has **Reliable 10** against objects and terrain.
- Ø **Shock Round: On Hit:** The target is Stunned and Slowed until the end of their next turn. **On Miss:** The target is Slowed and can't take **reactions** until the end of their next turn.

*Gunslingers carry specialized ammunition, developed to support their unique strengths and weaknesses. This ammunition can't be mass-produced because it contains volatile or experimental materials.*

Gunslingers lean towards weapons with the **Reloading** tag, as their traits helps them mitigate that penalty. Increasing their MP pool can also allow them to reload more often. While Gunslingers are naturally drawn toward long-range combat, their base traits can also work with melee weapons.

### HEXBLADE STRIKER / CONTROLLER

Hexblades sow curses and confusion among their foes, weakening them while growing stronger from their misery.

H exblades know pain and misery better than most. These warriors can take the suffering in their heart and use it to power an array of curses and hexes. To a hexblade, the soul is just another power source.

Hexes are blights upon creatures with aetheric fields. The simplest of them induce minor aetheric imbalances, causing clumsiness and inability to focus. The strongest ones act like magical parasites, using their hosts own aether to produce ongoing, detrimental effects like loss of vision and internal decay.

- Ø What's the worst luck you had?
- Ø How does the shape and appearance of your soul alter your weapons and techniques?
- Ø What's your favorite curse to put on people?
- Ø How do you get under people's skin?

Hexblades mix weapon and spell attacks together. They excel when they focus on taking down one target at a time or on spreading conditions to multiple targets. Because Hex counts as a spell attack, they make good use of the **Spellblade** talent.

### KNIGHTMARE DEFENDER/ CONTROLLER

Knightmares manifest dreams into reality, creating unbreakable aetheric chains to bind their foes. If a Knightmare catches you, there's no escape.

T he Dreamscape is a realm few know about, and accessible to even fewer. The Awoken are those who can travel this realm of infinite creativity and shape it to their whims; however, not everything there is pleasant, and dangers lurk in the shadows.

Knightmares have been influenced by their brushes with the horrors of the Dreamscape – true nightmares. Using these strong negative emotions, they can take objects from the Dreamscape into the waking world, which display properties that make a mockery of reality.

- Ø When did you first become Awoken?
- Ø How do you choose to look in the Dreamscape?
- Ø Who do you hope to capture in your chains?
- Ø What nightmare still plagues you to this day?

#### TRAITS

#### **The Chains of Despair** Trait

You gain the Chains of Despair skill, which is equipped for free.

### **Chains of Despair** Reaction

Skill Focus 1, Innate, Unique

**Trigger:** An enemy within Range 3 makes an attack.

**Effect:** You **pull** the enemy to the closest free space adjacent to you, and their attack must include you as a target. You may also spend an additional +1 Focus to Grapple the enemy after **pulling** them.

#### **The Inescapable Nightmare** Trait

When you would be able to make a melee attack as a **reaction**, you can instead attempt a Grapple.

Additionally, 1/round if a Grappled enemy would cease to be adjacent to you, you can spend 1 MP to continue the grapple and negate any movement that would break adjacency.

You can't use this trait if you are Stunned.

#### **The Weight of Inevitability** Trait

You are always considered at least Size 3 for the purposes of Grapple and Shove and you can perform **reactions** while Grappled.

WEAPON SLOTS LIGHT MAIN HEAVY SUPPORT SLOTS MAIN HEAVY

#### LIMIT BREAK

#### **Trapped in a Dream** Grapple Limit Break

All enemies of your choice within Range 5 are **pulled** to the closest free spaces adjacent to you and Grappled. Escaping from the grapple requires a **standard action** during any phase instead of a minor action. Any time an enemy Grappled by this ability attacks, they must include you as one of their targets.

*Your chains move according to your will, wrapping around your foes and dragging them screaming toward you. As they move closer, they find themselves transported into a domain of the dreamscape chosen especially for them. They can see the waking world, but the dreamscape fights them every step of the way.*

Knightmares are a grapple-focused class. The **Brawler** talent can help them Grapple more often, and grappling tends to shut down enemy defenders, who usually rely on **reactions** to use their defensive features. The Weight of Inevitability means that Knightmares don't need to pick specific ancestries to remain in control while grappling.

# RANK 1

#### **Body Shields**

Skill 1 Memory Unique

Any Physical, Astral, Lunar, and Force you would take is reduced by –1 (before Armor and **resistances**) for every character Grappled by you. When characters Grappled by you deal damage to you, they don't count toward this damage reduction.

You don't benefit from this ability while Sundered.

*Most people are about the size of a tower shield. They also do a pretty good job at blocking things – like a shield – even if they're not happy about it.*

#### **Night Terrors** Fight

Melee Spell Attack 1 Memory Aetheric, Mana 1 Reach 2 2 Stress

**On Hit:** Choose one of the following:

- Ø **The One Where You're Being Chased:** The target receives **+1** on all attacks that don't include you as the target. This stacks to a maximum of **+3** . If they deal damage or Stress to you, they clear this effect.
- Ø **The One Where Your Teeth Fall Out:** If the target is Breaking, they take 4 Stress instead of the usual Stress.

*You find the remnants of night terrors still lingering in the corners of a person's mind and make them relive those dreams.*

## RANK 2

#### **Dream Eater**

Skill 1 Memory Unique

**1/turn**, when an adjacent character gains **Stress**, you gain 1 Focus.

*As your foes become tired and stressed, you start to see the outlines of the nightmares that plague them. Turn these nightmares into your strength.*

#### **Recurring Dream**

Support Item Main Enchantment

You may use one of the following **Enchantments** at a time:

- Ø **Any Weapon:** When you hit with this weapon, as long as you are attacking the same target, you can Volley, Fight, or Full Attack (**Slow** weapons only) one phase earlier than before in subsequent rounds. This effect stacks, but cannot shift the attack any earlier than Phase Bolster. It lasts until the end of the scene or until you attack a different target with this weapon.
- Ø **Any Technique that Forces a Save: 1/turn** when a target succeeds on a save triggered by this technique, your Save Target increases by +1 for this technique only until the end of the scene or until a target fails the save, at which point it resets back to normal. This effect can stack.

*"Haven't we been here before? It feels so familiar…"*

## RANK 3

#### **Delirium**

Skill 2 Memory Unique

Whenever you take damage, you can take 1 Stress to lower the total needed for you to critically hit on attacks by 1 (i.e., from 20+ to 19+, 19+ to 18+, etc). This effect stacks and lasts until the end of the scene or until you critically hit.

*Give into your own nightmares, and let them take over for a bit.*

#### **Wraith Chain** Full Attack

Melee Weapon Heavy Longarm Aetheric, Pull 2, Slow, Thrown 5

Reach 2 / Thrown 5 3d6+1 Force

**On Hit:** If you are adjacent to the target or this attack **pulls** them adjacent to you, you may **auto-hit** on a Grapple against them.

You can attack with this weapon as a **minor action** during any phase, but you can only attack with it 1/ turn. If you attack with it as a minor action, it deals no damage.

*This weapon is wrapped in dream-chains, brought from the dreamscape into the waking world. Their strength is unparalleled, unbound by the constraints of reality. With a flick of your wrist, the chain shoots out and binds your victim, pulling them close.*

# LIFEWEAVER

SUPPORT

Lifeweavers are battlefield medics who weave flesh together. They can keep their friends active and fighting even through deadly injuries.

L ifeweavers have unique insights into the dynamics of aetheric fields and can use magic to heal wounds instantly. This knowledge also makes them dangerous fighters, as they know exactly how to hurt or incapacitate foes and turn their own aetheric fields against them.

These healers have honed their training for the battlefield. Their healing magic may not be as effective as proper medical attention, but it serves its purpose of quickly repairing minor injuries and letting people continue fighting.

- Ø What mark is left behind on those you've healed?
- Ø What type of effects or injuries do you find difficult to fix?
- Ø What do aetheric fields look like to you, and how is yours different?
- Ø What is the worst injury from which you've saved someone?

#### **Healer's Burden** Trait

When an ally spends or loses a Recovery within Range 5 or during a **Short Rest**, you can allow them to use your Recoveries instead.

#### **Quick Stitch** Reaction Trait Mana 1

**Trigger:** A Bloodied ally within Scope starts their turn.

**Effect:** You heal them for 1d6 HP.

**Purge Malady** Minor Action Trait Mana 2 Range = Scope

You or an ally within Range clear a condition.

WEAPON SLOTS MAIN

SUPPORT SLOTS LIGHT LIGHT

#### LIMIT BREAK

**Second Life** Channel Limit Break Arcing

If you stop channeling this ability before releasing it for any reason, you do not expend the limit break.

**On Release:** Choose either an ally within Scope or name a defeated ally. They are restored to their maximum HP; regain all Recoveries and MP; clear all **Stress**, conditions, and **Discord**; and clear two Wounds and two Overstresses. If they were defeated, they return to play in a free space adjacent to you.

*The ability to bring someone back from the dead, no matter how long has passed, exists only in legend. However, there is a very short window of time where life can be restored to the recently departed.*

*A lifeweaver can repair and stimulate a person's aetheric field before it completely breaks down, breathing life back into their body and repairing some of the recent damage they've taken.*

Lifeweavers have many ways of keeping their allies healthy during fights, from granting temp HP to pure healing. Lifeweavers play a versatile supporting role, especially when combined with other classes like the **Tidecaller**.

**Soul Shield** Phase Skirmish Spell 2 Memory Range = Scope

A character within Range gains 1d3+1 temp HP. You can spend 1 MP to increase this to 1d6+1 temp HP.

While the target has temp HP, they don't need to make **concentration saves** as long as they had the temp HP when the save was prompted.

*Strengthen and enhance an ally's aetheric field by drawing power from their own lifeforce.*

**Stolen Life** Volley Ranged Spell Attack 2 Memory Aetheric, Indirect Range = Scope 2 Stress

**On Hit:** Choose one of the following:

- Ø **Drain Life:** You or a character within Range 5 of you regains 1d3 HP. You can spend 1 MP to increase this to 1d6 HP instead.
- Ø **Drain Vitality:** You or a character within Range 5 of you clear up to 2 Stress.

*Siphon life energy from a victim and transfer it to someone else.*

**Inner Balance** Channel Spell 1 Memory Mana 2 Range = Scope

**On Release:** Choose one of the following:

- Ø **Balance:** Choose either yourself and one ally within Range or two allies within Range. The targets swap their current HP, Stress, or MP, your choice.
- Ø **Imbalance:** Choose an enemy within Range and roll 2d6, adding +1d6 if they are either Bloodied or Breaking or +2d6 if they are both. They take **Stress** equal to the result of one die, and **Piercing** Force equal to another, your choice.

*Lifeweavers seek to keep a harmonious balance between the mind, body, and soul. By drawing from another's aetheric field, a lifeweaver can use both fields to balance each other out or induce an internal imbalance.*

**Sewing Needle** Volley Ranged Weapon Main Artifact Piercing Range 5 / Reach 3 1d3+2 Force

**On Hit:** If the target is Bloodied, you can spend 1 MP to make them Immobilized until the end of their next turn. If you do so, any Bloodied enemies of your choice adjacent to the target are Slowed until the end of their next turn.

*A tool used to stitch flesh together, turned into a weapon. The needle itself is rarely seen. Instead, you catch glimpses of the shimmering magical thread.*

Spell 3 Memory Mana 1 Range = Scope

One character within Range can spend a Recovery to regain all HP. If you spend 3 MP instead, they don't need to spend a Recovery.

*The Sungarde, divinely-inspired swashbucklers, have enchanted their ships to make them permanent zones of serenity. People rescued by the Sungarde recover incredibly quickly, which has earned the swashbucklers a stellar reputation. The innocent cheer at the sight of Sungarde flags, and pirates flee from them.*

### PALADIN DEFENDER / SUPPORT

Paladins draw upon their resolve to protect their allies and smite their foes. They can manifest beneficial auras that enhance nearby allies.

P aladins are motivated individuals driven by their beliefs. What separates a paladin from an advocate is their ability to turn their resolve into power, bolstering their aetheric field and gaining strength through their convictions.

Most paladins are very protective of their friends and will willingly put themselves in harm's way to save one from danger. Their desire to protect becomes so strong that when they sense a threat, they can travel great distances in the blink of an eye to stand ready against it.

- Ø Who or what would you give your life to protect?
- Ø What is a belief you hold onto strongly?
- Ø What sensation or emotion do people feel when they're near you?
- Ø When did you stand up for something, and what did it cost you?

#### TRAITS

#### **Layered Auras** Trait

You gain the Aura of Inspiration spell, which is equipped for free. Additionally, you may have two Auras active at once, but they must be from different abilities.

#### **Aura of Inspiration** Free Action

Spell Arcing, Aura 5, Innate, Unique

You and all allies within this **Aura** gain **+1** on checks and saves.

### **Guardian Angel** Minor Action

Trait

Choose an ally within line of sight to protect. You may Interpose for them as long as they are within Scope, **teleporting** to a free adjacent space. This lasts for the rest of the scene or until you use this ability again.

#### **Retribution** Trait

You can Counterattack **2/round** instead of **1/round**.

Additionally, **1/round** when performing Interpose, you can Counterattack the attacker before the triggering attack happens. This Counterattack doesn't cost Focus.

WEAPON SLOTS MAIN HEAVY

#### LIMIT BREAK

#### **Bastion of Resolve** Special Limit Break

At any time during a combat scene, you may harden your resolve and surpass your normal limits. When activated, you clear all conditions and gain the following benefits, which last until the end of this phase next round:

- Ø You gain **resistance to all damage**.
- Ø Your weapons with Reach gain +2 Reach.
- Ø Your allies count as being under the protection of your Guardian Angel ability.

*Paladins draw upon their beliefs and convictions to strengthen their aetheric fields. The more deeply they believe in something, the stronger the effects. Once they've perfected this skill, the only limit on their power is the strength of their resolve. As long as they stand unwavering, they can't be stopped.*

As defenders, Paladins benefit from high Armor and other damage-mitigation effects. With their **Auras** and Retribution trait, they work best when close to their allies. The **Knight** talent is a good choice for Paladins.

- Ø **Aura of Freedom:** You and all allies within this **Aura** gain **immunity** to Slowed and can automatically break out of grapples as a **free action**.

*Expand your aetheric field to cover those of your allies. As they overlap, you can imbue their fields with your own protections.*

You or an adjacent ally can spend a Recovery to regain HP equal to their Bloodied value.

### PHOENIX ARTILLERY

Phoenixes conjure fiery destruction from afar, inflicting lingering burns upon their enemies.

P hoenixes are elite spellcasters specializing in elemental fire. During training, they take the mythical phoenix as their inspiration, focusing on the different aspects of fire, such as destruction and rebirth. To become a phoenix, you must give over your aetheric field to the raw forces of flame. If you have prepared correctly, it will be returned infused with fire; if not, your aether will be consumed.

- Ø What color and shape do your flames take?
- Ø What aspect of fire do you respect the most? Destruction, warmth, rebirth, or something else?
- Ø How has fire affected or shaped your life?
- Ø What makes your connection to fire stronger, and what makes it weaker?

#### TRAITS

# **Burning Spells** Trait You deal **+1 Discord** with all abilities that deal **Discord**. **Burn Proof** Trait You have **resistance to Astral and Discord**

#### LIMIT BREAK

**Supervolcano** Channel Limit Break Homing, Mana 2 Range = Scope

If you stop channeling this ability before releasing it for any reason, you do not expend the limit break.

**On Release:** You create a Size 3 volcano – a piece of terrain – within Range. The volcano destroys any objects (including terrain) other than objectives in its spaces. Characters in these spaces are considered to be atop the volcano.

Mark 6 spaces as **lava flows**. These must be adjacent to either the volcano or another lava flow space. Lava flows are **dangerous terrain** (Agility, 5 Discord ).

Characters within or adjacent to the volcano or a lava flow space receive **+2** on checks to clear **Discord**.

On subsequent turns, you can spend 1 MP as a **minor action** to add or remove up to 3 lava flow spaces.

The volcano and lava flows last until the end of the scene.

*Reaching deep below the surface with aetheric might, the phoenix pulls up a grand manifestation of burning power – a raging volcano. Lava erupts from within, forming molten rivers that burn those who stray too close.*

Phoenixes make expert use of effects that deal **Discord**. Their significant MP and long Scope means they are well suited to attack safely from afar with spells. The **Sniper** talent is a good fit for Phoenixes that use ranged spell attacks.

### RIFTBLADE ARTILLERY

Armed with experimental blades that can cut rifts in space-time itself, riftblades can travel between Reflections to explore new worlds.

R iftblades explore the different Reflections of the Source, cataloging as much about these new and interesting worlds as possible. The main tool of a riftblade is their signature riftcutter, a magitech device with an infinitely sharp edge that can cut small portals between Reflections. Knowing that each new Reflection is filled with unknown challenges and hazards, riftblades outfit themselves with a wide array of gear to prepare for any situation.

- Ø What was the most interesting Reflection you visited?
- Ø What do you hope to find in your travels?
- Ø What did you leave behind in a rift you'll never be able to get back?
- Ø Where are you forbidden to open a rift to?

#### TRAITS

**Rift** Minor Action Trait Mana 1 Range 20

You place a **rift** in a free space within Range. This doesn't require line of sight.

Range, Reach, and line of sight for **everything** (except placing additional rifts) can be drawn **both to and from you** as if you were standing in the rift's space. The rift's space is considered occupied by you.

The rift itself is not an object and cannot be directly interacted with or affected in any way. It lasts for the rest of the scene, until you use this ability again, or until you dismiss it as a **minor action**.

**Riftcutter** Trait

You gain the Riftcutter weapon, which is equipped for free.

**Riftcutter** Fight Melee Weapon Light Magitech Accurate, Innate, Piercing, Unique Reach 1 3 Physical

This weapon ignores **resistance** and **immunity**.

WEAPON SLOTS SUPPORT SLOTS LIGHT HEAVY LIGHT LIGHT LIGHT MAIN

#### LIMIT BREAK

#### **Gateway** Minor Action Limit Break

You create two Size 3 gateways on free solid surfaces within Range 15 , ignoring line of sight. These gateways aren't obstructions, don't block line of sight, and last until the end of the scene.

**1/turn** when a character fully enters a gateway, they are immediately **teleported** to the other gateway, being placed in a space of their choice so they are fully within the gateway. If a character enters a gateway as a result of forced movement, their forced movement continues in the same direction from the other gateway starting in a space chosen by whoever initiated the forced movement.

If a character cannot end this **teleport** fully within the gateway (e.g., because too many spaces are occupied), the **teleport** fails.

*Riftcutters can be overcharged with energy, allowing them to create larger and more stable rifts. With the proper architecture, these gateways can become permanent portals, allowing instant travel between two places; however, the portable power packs that riftblades carry aren't strong enough, and the gateways they create will eventually close.*

The Riftblade is a complicated class that use their Rift trait to gain range and angles on their enemies. Careful positioning of a rift is necessary, as enemies can attack them through it. Try placing rifts in cover or where enemies can't reach.

*Gravity generators are a standard piece of equipment for Riftblades. The ability to create a comfortable gravity field around themselves is sometimes the difference between life and death.*

Additionally, **1/turn**, you can reroll the Invisible miss chance when attacking an Invisible target.

*These goggles are equipped with aetheric and biometric scanners for any situation.*

### RIMEGUARD DEFENDER

Icy armor grows over rimeguards, granting layers of protection that grow stronger over time.

T he Rimeguard's source of power is their frozen heart. A splinter of ice formed from the death of an ancient elemental spirit is driven into their heart, encasing it in ice and freezing the blood in their veins. Those that survive the process become one with the forces of cold.

The icy splinters needed to create a rimeguard are rare, and as a result, rimeguards are an uncommon sight. Their rarity combined with their cold and distant demeanor, gives rimeguards a frightening reputation that is only sometimes earned.

- Ø Who did you save from certain death?

Rimeguards want to generate as much Focus as possible, allocating it between temp HP granted by Frost Armor and their other Focus needs. Because of their unique mechanism for stacking temp HP, rimeguards can either keep the temp HP for themselves and adopt an interposing defender style, or play the role of the backline defender by sharing temp HP with allies.

### SEEKER ARTILLERY / CONTROLLER

Trackers and hunters that always find their prey. Seekers call out priority targets for their allies.

S eekers are warriors who form bonds with primal spirits, which impart upon them a fraction of their power. In return for power, seekers act as extensions of their patron spirits, operating beyond their normal boundaries.

Most seekers have a positive relationship with their bonded primal, but some forged their bonds out of desperation or necessity. These seekers may not agree with their primal's motives, but are nonetheless compelled to do its bidding or risk losing their powers. However antagonistic the relationship, the seeker acts as the eyes, ears, and hands of their primal.

- Ø How do you see and know the exact spots to shoot?
- Ø What primal spirit do you have a connection with?
- Ø Which of your primal spirit's aspects do you share?
- Ø What is the most dangerous expedition you've been on?

# SHADEBINDER

CONTROLLER

Shadebinders capture and train dangerous shades, forging strong bonds with them.

S udden shifts in an area's aetheric balance can give rise to shades – creatures made of pure aether. These creatures can be dangerous, often lashing out in pain and confusion due to the environmental imbalance.

Shadebinders capture and train these dangerous shades, returning them to their homes once the imbalance is addressed, or forging strong bonds with them and becoming traveling companions.

- Ø What was the first shade you trapped, and what were they like?
- Ø What was the most elaborate trap you set, and what was it for?
- Ø When were you unable to return a shade to its home, and why?
- Ø What aetheric disturbance has never been repaired, and is only getting worse?

Shadebinders are a summoning class that prioritizes having fewer, stronger **Summons**. Command Partner lets their **Summons** take actions on the behalf of the Shadebinder, like grappling enemies instead of doing it themselves. As with most summoners, they will likely want to take the **Summoner** talent.

Once during each of your turns, one of your **Summons** within Scope can use one of your actions against a target within your Scope. You lose the action and pay any costs, like MP or **Consumable** charges, but the **Summon** is considered to be the one performing it. If it is a Channel action, the **Summon** is the one that concentrates and can release during Phase Release.

While performing an action using this trait, your **Summon** benefits from your talents.

You can only have one shade in play at a time and it retains its current HP when dismissed, which persists between scenes. If the shade is defeated, you must spend a Recovery to summon it again, after which it regains all HP. It regains all HP during **Rests**.

# SHADOW DANCER

STRIKER

Shadow dancers cloak themselves with darkness, moving unseen across the battlefield. Their special connection with the shadows allows them to teleport between areas of darkness.

S hadow dancers train in the dark, increasing their attunement to the power of the umbra. When they finish their training, their shadow has changed: it moves differently, feels looser, and seems darker. Every shadow looks different to the dancer once they realize that they're all connected.

- Ø Why do you feel more comfortable in the dark?

*Umbral shadows creep over any source of light, plunging the area into darkness. Nothing is safe from the obscurement, not even the sun itself.*

Shadow Dancers are high-mobility attackers, moving across the battlefield to attack precisely when they need to before disappearing into a pool of darkness. The **Assassin** class has useful stealth-enhancing abilities, and any teleportation effects give the Shadow Dancer more opportunities to gain Shadow Clones.

# SHAPESHIFTER

<span id="page-177-0"></span>SUPPORT

Shapeshifters are able to assume various forms to overcome obstacles, transforming their entire body or individual parts as needed.

S hapeshifters can change their bodies, taking on the features of other creatures or changing into different creatures altogether. The earliest shapeshifters were lycanthropes studying the way their transformations worked. Over time, they discovered how to transform specific parts of their bodies, and eventually how to choose what they transformed into.

This type of shapeshifting is notable because it's a learned skill, not a spell. The shapeshifter carefully rearranges their aetheric balance until it precisely aligns with the typical aetheric balance of the creature they're shapeshifting into. While their form may be different, they're still the same person they were before. A shapeshifter that transforms into a bear isn't actually a bear; they're merely adopting the outward form of a bear.

- Ø What does it look like when you transform?
- Ø What physical trait carries over to each form you assume?
- Ø When was the first time you changed your shape, and what was it?
- Ø What form or changes feel most natural to you?

#### TRAITS

#### **Beast Weapons** Trait

You gain Beast Weapons, which is equipped for free.

#### **Beast Weapons** Fight

Melee Weapon Light Club Innate, Unique Reach 1 1d3 Physical

At the end of your turn, if you didn't make an attack that turn, you can attack once with this weapon as a **free action**.

You can use this weapon even if Disarmed.

#### **Form for the Occasion** Trait

You move normally in **difficult terrain**. Additionally, you have an **Aquatic Adaptation** and you can **fly** when you move, but you take 1 Stress at the end of any turn in which you do so.

#### **Soothe** Trait

When an ally within Scope consumes a Mark, you can clear **1 Stress** from them as long as they aren't Stunned.

WEAPON SLOTS LIGHT HEAVY

#### LIMIT BREAK

#### **Perfect Form** Free Action Limit Break

You gain up to four of the following benefits, which last until the end of the scene. **1/turn** as a **free action**, you can change up to four of your chosen benefits. Only one instance of each option can be in effect at a time:

- Ø **Adaptive Body:** You gain **+1** on all checks and saves.
- Ø **Dire Beast Weapons:** Your Beast Weapons deal 1d6+2 Damage instead of their standard damage.
- Ø **Eagle Eye:** You can Mark as a **minor action**.
- Ø **Perfected Form: Form for the Occasion** no longer causes Stress when you fly, and you have **flight**.
- Ø **Hunter's Eye:** At the start of your turn, you gain 1 Focus.
- Ø **Insulated Hide:** You gain **resistance to Physical**, **Astral, Lunar, Force, Stress, or Discord** from other characters.
- Ø **Predator Weapons:** You gain **+1** on attacks.
- Ø **Resized:** You become any Size from 1/2 to 4. Characters are **pushed** to make space for your new size.
- Ø **Sneaky:** You can Hide as a **minor action**.
- Ø **Walking Wall:** You count as **hard cover** for your allies.

*Take the best parts of every form you know, and manifest them all at once. As long as you can maintain them, you are the ultimate lifeform.*

A shapeshifter's different forms make them very versatile in combat play, and with Form for the Occasion they don't need to worry about changing their equipment and techniques based on the environment. Shapeshifters are a jack-of-all-trades class, and can adopt a range of playstyles by combining their shapeshifting forms with those from other classes.

### SKALD SUPPORT

Skalds are warrior poets who weave magic into song. A skald's songs can bolster their allies, providing powerful benefits.

T here is magic in a song. There is magic in a dance. Any performance where you put some of yourself into it is magic. Skalds know this, and can push it to its extremes. Stories come to life and move people, or draw out specific emotions. Performance is the weapon of the skald.

A skald's performances stimulate the aetheric field of nearby creatures. By honing these performances, they learn to produce specific, repeatable effects. Skalds enjoy traveling and meeting other skalds, growing their repertoire of performances and related magical effects.

- Ø What type of performance is your specialty?
- Ø What weapon and instrument have you combined? Songbow, hammerhorn, or something else?
- Ø What does your entrance music sound like, or what is the main instrument it features?
- Ø Who was moved by your performance that you didn't expect to be?

#### **Encore** Trait

#### **Inspire** Free Action (1/turn) Trait Mana 1 Range = Scope

You grant **Inspiration** to an ally within Range. Allies with **Inspiration** can consume it to gain **+1** on an attack, check, or save. Each character can only have one **Inspiration** at a time. **Inspiration** lasts until the end of the scene or until consumed.

### **Tempo** Trait

You gain the Tempo skill, which is equipped for free.

SUPPORT SLOTS LIGHT MAIN

#### LIMIT BREAK

**Ballad** Free Action Limit Break Arcing Range = Scope

You begin weaving a legendary performance. Upon activation and at the start of each of your turns, pick a **verse** from those listed below. It affects you and all allies within Range. Each verse can only be chosen once.

Instead of picking a verse (or when there are no more verses to select), you may select a **finale** from those listed below. It affects you and all allies within Range, following which this ability ends.

If you are Silenced, the effects of all verses are suppressed, and you don't choose any new verses or a finale until you have cleared the condition.

#### **VERSES:**

- Ø **Verse of Heroes: +1** on attack rolls.
- Ø **Verse of Journeys:** +2 Speed.
- Ø **Verse of Relaxation:** Gain **resistance to Stress**.
- Ø **Verse of Teamwork: +1** on all checks and saves.

#### **FINALE:**

- Ø **The Last Stand:** Gain 1d6+4 Focus.
- Ø **The Storm Fades:** Regain all MP.
- Ø **Legends Never Die:** Regain all HP.
- Ø **A New Beginning:** Clear all **Stress** and conditions.
- Ø **The New World:** Each character can **shift** up to their Speed.

*A skald can weave so much aether into a performance that it becomes real. The audience feels the stories come to life. they feel the emotions of the players; their hopes and dreams.*

*Each ballad tells a specific story, each of its acts mirror whatever is happening in the moment. Eventually, your actions become inseparable from those in the story, and are memorialized in all future retellings.*

Skalds have many ways to support their allies, from the free movement of Tempo to the accuracy of Inspire. They are efficient users of Channel abilities, using Encore to chain together channeling abilities each round.

### THUNDERCLAW STRIKER

Thunderclaws have mastered electricity. They use it to enhance their physical abilities, giving them explosive speed and power.

T hunderclaws are named after the elite canine warriors that created the fighting style. Training atop a mountain in the Thunderfields, they gained unique insights into the properties of lightning. They learned how to generate and channel it through their bodies, and how to hold it within to enhance their physical abilities.

- Ø What is unique about your fighting style?
- Ø Where did you train, and why can't anyone else train there now?
- Ø What is the most dangerous technique you know, and why is it dangerous?
- Ø What does it feel like to hold lightning within yourself?

#### LIMIT BREAK

#### **Living Lightning** Free Action Limit Break

You channel lightning directly into your body, pushing it past its physical limits and immediately gaining **6 Flurry**. Until the end of the scene, your **Fast as Lightning** trait doesn't cost MP. When you spend **6 Flurry** to make a **Stunning Strike**, the target automatically fails the save.

*A bolt of magical lightning strikes the thunderclaw, but instead of discharging it, they hold it within their body. Years of careful training allow the thunderclaw to take this influx of energy and use it to enhance their physical abilities.*

*Supercharged thunderclaws can dart across the battlefield at breakneck speeds, delivering lightningcharged strikes strong enough to punch through steel. Lightning arcs off their body while in this state, as they contain the raw power of a bolt of lightning.*

*Held lightning still wants to find a way out of a thunderclaw's body, and the longer it is held, the greater the damage to the thunderclaw. If not discharged, this technique will eventually kill the user.*

Thunderclaws are highly mobile, with abilities that allow them to quickly move around a battlefield, and others that require specific positioning to maximize their effects. The more attacks a Thunderclaw makes, the more **Flurry** they can generate, so the **Stormdancer** talent's third rank and other abilities that allow for extra attacks are good choices for this class.

### TIDECALLER CONTROLLER / SUPPORT

Tidecallers command the oceans and waters. They can manipulate currents to reposition allies and hinder enemies.

C ommon wisdom suggests that only a fool braves the seas without a tidecaller. Their command over water and their insight into weather patterns make them invaluable additions to any seafaring vessel.

Tidecallers are strongly associated with water-aspect aether, known for its healing properties. A drop of this magical water can heal a small cut, while a deluge can repair deadly wounds and restore vitality. An often-forgotten aspect of water-aspect aether is its weight. Surprisingly, small quantities can knock a grown person off their feet and drag them around in its wake.

- Ø What secret do you know about the ocean?
- Ø How do you accurately predict the weather?
- Ø What underwater-dwelling person do you owe a favor to?
- Ø How do people typically travel across open water?

#### LIMIT BREAK

### Limit Break

**Tidal Wave** Phase Skirmish

Choose one side of the map. A wave of water washes towards that side from the other, and all enemies must make Bulk saves. **Failure:** They are **pushed** 6 spaces in a straight line towards the chosen side of the map, are Dazed and Disarmed until the end of their next turn, and are knocked Prone. **Success:** They are **pushed** half as many spaces and are knocked Prone.

Enemies that hit a character or obstruction when **pushed** by this ability take 5 Physical and are Sundered until they clear the condition.

*Tidecallers have control over the seas, but even when far away from the ocean, their power is no less deadly.*

*When there isn't water around to command, a tidecaller can converts the ambient aether in the atmosphere to water aether, drawing the moisture out of everything to form a roaring wave.*

Tidecallers use spells to move their allies and enemies around the battlefield with Rising Tide and River Current, while providing healing support with spells like Tidal Healing. Their rank 3 Whirlpool ability is incredibly strong in combat scenes that require controlling an area, like **Acquisition** or **Control**.

### WARDEN DEFENDER

The earth heeds the call of the wardens, rising to restrict their foes while the wardens endure with the planet's lifeforce.

W ardens are connected to the lifeforce of the planet, controlling the elements of earth and rock. They feel what the planet feels, and when it cries out in pain, they rise to defend it. In return, they can draw upon the strength of the planet itself to protect themselves.

Wardens can connect and attune to any planet or plane to which they travel, although doing so may change how their powers look or work. Traveling to a dimension of fire, for example, a warden might find their hair turning to flame, and their powers becoming wreathed in flame.

- Ø What earthlike features do you have? Rocky skin, stone hair, something else?
- Ø What creature or spirit did you learn from?
- Ø How does the planet communicate with you?
- Ø What are the unique properties of the tree from which one of your pieces of equipment is made?

### WARLORD SUPPORT

Warlords are commanders of the battlefield. They lead their allies to victory, making them better fighters in the process.

T acticians and leaders, warlords are the glue that holds their groups together. They feel the rhythm of battle, adjusting their plans, tactics, and people accordingly.

Warlords can either be backline strategists, supporting their allies with intelligence and positioning, or frontline commanders, leading by example in the thick of battle.

- Ø When did one of your decisions save the day?
- Ø What tactical mistake have you promised to never let happen again?

You plant a standard in a free adjacent space. It creates a Burst 2 zone. You and your allies in the zone gain **+1** on attack rolls, checks, and saves, and when you or an ally start their turn in the zone, they gain 2 temp HP.

During your turn, you can pick up and move the standard when you move, placing it in another free adjacent space.

This **Summon** can only be dismissed with a **minor action** while adjacent to it. You can only have one standard in play at a time and it retains its current HP when dismissed. It regains all HP during **Rests**.

If defeated, this support item is destroyed.

*You carry a flag as a symbol of your unit. Those that see it know that any units near it fall under your command and protection.*

## RANK 2

#### **Frontline** Fight

Melee Weapon Main Longarm Reach 2 1d6+2 Physical

**On Crit:** You can **teleport** one willing ally within Scope up to **5 spaces**.

*Before battle, the troops gather around for a meal. As a surprise, their commander produces a treat – a deer they had hunted earlier in the day and prepared in the style of their homeland. As everyone enjoys the meal and camaraderie, their aetheric fields become attuned to the spear lying nearby.*

*The next day, in the heat of battle, the commander finds themselves separated from their troops. Facing certain doom, the commander readies their spear. In response, they feel aetheric energies beginning to rise from the spear, and then see a flash of light. The light fades, and the commander sees their troops stood before them, weapons raised and ready to fight.*

#### **Lead from the Front** Skill 1 Memory Unique

When you Fight or perform a Full Attack with a melee weapon, one ally of your choice within Scope gains 2 temp HP.

*You take to the battlefield, making your way to the frontlines. Your tactical prowess hadn't been questioned, but now neither will your martial prowess either.*

## RANK 3

#### **Focus Target**

Skill 3 Memory Stressful 2, Unique

**1/round** when an ally within Scope consumes a Mark, another ally of your choice within Scope can make a weapon or spell attack against the target as a **reaction**.

*It is more efficient to focus your attacks on one target at a time instead of spreading your blows around. If you can take out an enemy, they pose no threat to you. But a wounded enemy can still fight, and can wound you in return. So, do everyone a favor, and make sure they're dead before moving onto the next.*

### **Rally** Reaction

Skill 2 Memory

**Trigger:** An ally within Scope takes a **Wound** or **Overstress**.

**Effect:** No excess damage or **Stress** carries over, and your ally gains +4 temp HP and clears any **Discord** and conditions.

*"Rally to me! I will be your shield; I will hold the line! Their assault ends here!"*

# 8: TALENTS

Talents are bundles of abilities you can learn that aren't tied to specific classes. Each talent has three ranks. Beacons start with three talents at **rank 1**.

<span id="page-201-0"></span>Talents come in three ranks: **rank 1**, **rank 2**, and **rank 3**. Ranks must be taken in order; you need rank 1 in a talent to take rank 2, and you need rank 2 to take rank 3.

When you level up, you can increase the rank of an existing talent or gain a new one at rank 1.

Once you have a talent, its effects are always available to you.

### AETHERBENDER

Your magical expertise gives you insight into the manipulation of spells for different effects.

#### **Rank 1: Aetheric Upgrade**

You can apply the following effects to your spells. Each spell can only be affected by one at a time:

- Ø **Far Spell:** When you use a spell, you can spend 1 MP to do so with +5 Range.
- Ø **Fast Spell:** During your turn, you can use Volley or Fight spells during earlier phases, but no earlier than Phase Bolster. For each phase earlier than its normal phase, you spend 1 MP.
- Ø **Quiet Spell:** When you use a spell while Hidden, you can spend 2 MP to remain Hidden after the spell resolves.

#### **Rank 2: Advanced Aetheric Upgrade**

You add the following effects to your Aetheric Upgrades options:

- Ø **Flexible Spell:** When you make a spell attack, you can spend 1 MP to change the defense that it targets (Dodge or A-Def).
- Ø **Empowered Spell:** When you use a spell that involves a save, you can spend 2 MP to impose **+1** on any saves it causes.
- Ø **Repeated Spell:** When you make a non-AOE spell attack, you can spend 3 MP to attack two targets. They must be within Range 5 of each other and within your Scope.

#### **Rank 3: Aether Mixer**

You can apply two different Aetheric Upgrade options at once, spending the MP for each effect.

Additionally, you gain 1 MP when you crit with a spell attack.

### ARCHMAGE

Archmages stand at the pinnacle of aetheric research. They manipulate magic in ways that others can only dream of.

#### **Rank 1: Spell Sculptor**

When you use a spell or artifact weapon with an AOE, you can spend 1 MP to allow one character in the AOE to ignore the effect.

#### **Rank 2: Arcane Turret**

You gain the following benefits:

- Ø When you are channeling, you gain +2 Armor, to a maximum of 4.
- Ø Taking **Stress** doesn't trigger **concentration saves**.

#### **Rank 3: Focused Caster**

The following option is added to your **Reroll Table:**

Ø **Mana Effort:** Spend 1 MP.

### ARMORED DEFENDER

Armor keeps you alive. The stronger and more reliable your armor is, the longer you stay alive.

#### **Rank 1: Reinforced Plating**

After you Defend, you can spend Focus, temporarily gaining +1 Armor per Focus spent, to a maximum of 6 Armor. This lasts until the start of your next turn or until you next take Physical, Astral, Lunar, or Force.

#### **Rank 2: Adamantine Armor**

Your **Armor** support items become **Unbreakable**.

#### **Rank 3: Battle Hardened**

When you reduce a source of damage to 0, you negate all other effects the attack or ability would have on you, including on-hit effects.
